# Raw-literal ratchet for DesignTokenCheck.RunLocalization (spec 068 T012).
#
# Interface text still written as a plain string literal where Nota draws it, instead of going
# through Loc.T / Loc.F / Loc.Plural. It lists what existed when the check landed, so the check
# started green. A line may only be removed: a new raw literal fails the check, and so does a
# line that no longer matches anything. Wrap the literal in Loc.T and delete its line.
# When spec 068 is done, only "data:" lines remain: user content, device and parameter names,
# proper nouns and units, which are not translated.
#
# Regenerate after a bulk change with NOTA_LOC_WRITE_BASELINE=1 (reasons already given are kept).
#
# Format: path (from src/managed) <TAB> the literal exactly as written <TAB> # reason
